## Post-mortem

123 loss(es) and 128 won control(s) examined with the same rule.

| verdict | losses | wins (control) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| REASONING_WRONG | 0 | 0 |
| INFORMATION_MISSING | 0 | 0 |
| VARIANCE | 123 | 128 |

Read the two columns together. A verdict class that appears at the same rate among wins as among losses is describing the games we bet, not the bets we lost.
WARNING: every one of the 251 settled decisions examined landed in the same class (VARIANCE). This classifier separated NOTHING here -- do not read the column of losses as evidence about the reasoning.
87 pivot(s) were measured on the run-margin PROXY, not a win probability.

### What this suggests researching
- No signature cleared the pattern floor (>= 3 losses, >= 25% of losses, >= 2x the rate in the won control). These losses look like individual games, not a family.

A post-mortem is a description of what happened, never evidence for a strategy change (docs/RESEARCH_CATALOGUE.md T8: no rescue by threshold change).

### LOSS: 56ba4bb647b80640 / h2h home (game 824228)

Thesis: Backing the home side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) starter_velocity_gap -- how far the opposing starter's fastball sits above league pace (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 0.79 mph, home -1.59 mph (this game's own values rest on: away over 162 measured fastballs; home over 190 measured fastballs); a gap of 2.38 mph, clearing the threshold of 1.96 mph (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 3,149 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at -1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism harms), so the signal points to the home side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter whose fastball sits above league pace is holding stuff the season line has not caught up to, so the lineup facing the harder thrower is the disadvantaged side; velocity leads results and the market prices results. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 56ba4bb647b80640.)

**What happened.**
MIN 3 at DET 2; 66 plate appearances, 2 lead change(s).
Stopped being in doubt in the top of the 6th -- MLB win probability: the last play after which the winner's own win probability never fell below 50% again.

**What the pick promised, and what the game did.**
- UNDETERMINED `starter_velocity_gap@rung0`: promised the harder-throwing starter -- the one this side is backed BECAUSE its opponent has to face him -- holds that opposing lineup below the league rate starters allow (rule: reached_base_rate < 0.3245, over at least 9 observations); measured unmeasurable over 0 observation(s) of no play-by-play stored -- below the frozen sample floor or unmeasurable.

**The pivot.**
top 6th, runner on 1B, 1 out: Kody Clemens homers (25) on a fly ball to right center field. Luke Keaschall scores. Our side: win probability 66% -> 36% (-29%).
Worst half-inning: top 6th, 2 RBI -- win probability 66% -> 40% (-26%).
Metric: win_probability -- this bet settles on the full-game winner, which is exactly what MLB's win-probability series predicts.
Pitcher on the mound was the starter.

**Verdict: VARIANCE (mechanism_undetermined)**
- 1 mechanism check(s) recorded, none refuted, but 1 could not be decided by this game: ['starter_velocity_gap@rung0']
- settlement's own thesis_outcome: UNTESTED

**What this post-mortem could not determine.**
- this decision's frozen mechanism check(s) could not be decided by this game -- too few plate appearances against the starter, or no play-by-play stored. The claim was made and stands unchecked; that is not the same as it holding

### LOSS: ed2b9ec19d3b9b9a / h2h home (game 824228)

Thesis: Backing the home side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) top_minus_bottom -- this lineup's top-of-order minus bottom-of-order strength (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away -0.002 wOBA, home 0.028 wOBA (this game's own values rest on: away over 4,734 plate-appearance denominators; home over 2,272 plate-appearance denominators); a gap of 0.030 wOBA, clearing the threshold of 0.023 wOBA (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 4,838 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at +1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism favours), so the signal points to the home side. ### LOSS: market_derived_consensus_h2h_1st_5_innings_away / h2h_1st_5_innings away (game 824228)

Thesis: This pick carries the market's own probability and no edge, by construction. Its p_model is the board's own de-vigged consensus for this exact selection, republished unchanged by analyze()'s PROJECT phase under the identity map -- zero fitted parameters, no recalibration intercept, no Platt, no isotonic refit (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md §2). Because the probability IS the price, diffing the two would measure nothing: edge_bps is structurally None and no edge is claimed. It is published as a calibration reference -- how good the market's own number is -- never as a reason to bet.

This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at +1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism favours), so the signal points to the away side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter who leans on one pitch, against a lineup that has measurably hit that pitch, has nowhere to hide for eighteen outs; the market prices his season line, not tonight's specific collision. ### LOSS: 56ba4bb647b80640 / h2h home (game 823821)

Thesis: Backing the home side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) starter_velocity_gap -- how far the opposing starter's fastball sits above league pace (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 3.24 mph, home -2.07 mph (this game's own values rest on: away over 206 measured fastballs; home over 259 measured fastballs); a gap of 5.31 mph, clearing the threshold of 1.96 mph (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 3,149 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at -1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism harms), so the signal points to the home side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter whose fastball sits above league pace is holding stuff the season line has not caught up to, so the lineup facing the harder thrower is the disadvantaged side; velocity leads results and the market prices results. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 56ba4bb647b80640.)

**What happened.**
NYM 7 at MIA 5; 77 plate appearances, 0 lead change(s).
Stopped being in doubt in the top of the 1st -- MLB win probability: the last play after which the winner's own win probability never fell below 50% again.

**What the pick promised, and what the game did.**

### LOSS: dcedc80dd159bf62 / h2h away (game 823738)

Thesis: Backing the away side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) primary_pitch_share -- the share of pitches the opposing starter throws with his single most-used offering (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 60.4%, home 29.1% (this game's own values rest on: away over 3,542 pitches; home over 8,682 pitches); a gap of 31.3 percentage points, clearing the threshold of 23.1 percentage points (rung 3 of 3, the 90th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 4,061 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at +1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism favours), so the signal points to the away side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: concentration in one pitch is predictability: the more of a starter's arsenal is a single offering, the more of the lineup's preparation transfers, and pitch-level lean is not a term in any club-level price. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). **The pivot.**

### LOSS: 56ba4bb647b80640 / h2h home (game 823414)

Thesis: Backing the home side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) starter_velocity_gap -- how far the opposing starter's fastball sits above league pace (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 2.17 mph, home -0.92 mph (this game's own values rest on: away over 182 measured fastballs; home over 179 measured fastballs); a gap of 3.08 mph, clearing the threshold of 1.96 mph (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 3,149 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at -1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism harms), so the signal points to the home side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter whose fastball sits above league pace is holding stuff the season line has not caught up to, so the lineup facing the harder thrower is the disadvantaged side; velocity leads results and the market prices results. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 56ba4bb647b80640.)

**What happened.**

- UNDETERMINED `starter_velocity_gap@rung0`: promised the harder-throwing starter -- the one this side is backed BECAUSE its opponent has to face him -- holds that opposing lineup below the league rate starters allow (rule: reached_base_rate < 0.3245, over at least 9 observations); measured unmeasurable over 0 observation(s) of no play-by-play stored -- below the frozen sample floor or unmeasurable.

Metric: win_probability -- this bet settles on the full-game winner, which is exactly what MLB's win-probability series predicts.

### LOSS: 7be45f28a9c9312a / h2h home (game 824063)

Thesis: Backing the home side of h2h because 2 pre-registered signals fired: (1) lineup_vs_primary_pitch -- this lineup's measured production against the primary pitch of the starter it faces (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 0.295 wOBA, home 0.358 wOBA (this game's own values rest on: away over 1,493 plate appearances; home over 977 plate appearances); a gap of 0.063 wOBA, clearing the threshold of 0.053 wOBA (rung 2 of 3, the 75th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 4,048 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at +1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism favours), so the signal points to the home side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter who leans on one pitch, against a lineup that has measurably hit that pitch, has nowhere to hide for eighteen outs; the market prices his season line, not tonight's specific collision. (2) primary_pitch_share -- the share of pitches the opposing starter throws with his single most-used offering (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 29.1%, home 51.0% (this game's own values rest on: away over 10,138 pitches; home over 7,103 pitches); a gap of 21.9 percentage points, clearing the threshold of 9.7 percentage points (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 4,061 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at +1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism favours), so the signal points to the home side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: concentration in one pitch is predictability: the more of a starter's arsenal is a single offering, the more of the lineup's preparation transfers, and pitch-level lean is not a term in any club-level price. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 7be45f28a9c9312a.)

### WIN (WON -- control): 192eda5ca8760fce / h2h_1st_5_innings away (game 824792)

Thesis: Backing the away side of h2h_1st_5_innings because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) lineup_platoon_share -- the share of the posted lineup holding the platoon advantage over the starter it faces (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 83.3%, home 57.1% (this game's own values rest on: away over 9 lineup slots; home over 9 lineup slots); a gap of 26.2 percentage points, clearing the threshold of 22.2 percentage points (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 4,856 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at +1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism favours), so the signal points to the away side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: the classic exploitation: a lineup posted one-handed against a starter it holds the platoon advantage over gets more of its plate appearances in the favourable split than the club-level season line the market prices ever reflects. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 192eda5ca8760fce.)

**What happened.**
CLE 9 at BAL 5; 82 plate appearances, 0 lead change(s).

Thesis: Backing the home side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) top_minus_bottom -- this lineup's top-of-order minus bottom-of-order strength (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 0.040 wOBA, home 0.083 wOBA (this game's own values rest on: away over 4,262 plate-appearance denominators; home over 1,236 plate-appearance denominators); a gap of 0.043 wOBA, clearing the threshold of 0.042 wOBA (rung 2 of 3, the 75th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 4,838 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). ### WIN (WON -- control): 56ba4bb647b80640 / h2h home (game 823738)

Thesis: Backing the home side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) starter_velocity_gap -- how far the opposing starter's fastball sits above league pace (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 6.35 mph, home -1.47 mph (this game's own values rest on: away over 282 measured fastballs; home over 207 measured fastballs); a gap of 7.81 mph, clearing the threshold of 1.96 mph (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 3,149 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at -1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism harms), so the signal points to the home side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter whose fastball sits above league pace is holding stuff the season line has not caught up to, so the lineup facing the harder thrower is the disadvantaged side; velocity leads results and the market prices results. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 56ba4bb647b80640.)

**What happened.**
CHC 3 at MIL 4; 83 plate appearances, 1 lead change(s).
Stopped being in doubt in the top of the 10th -- MLB win probability: the last play after which the winner's own win probability never fell below 50% again.

**What the pick promised, and what the game did.**
- UNDETERMINED `starter_velocity_gap@rung0`: promised the harder-throwing starter -- the one this side is backed BECAUSE its opponent has to face him -- holds that opposing lineup below the league rate starters allow (rule: reached_base_rate < 0.3245, over at least 9 observations); measured unmeasurable over 0 observation(s) of no play-by-play stored -- below the frozen sample floor or unmeasurable.

**The pivot.**
top 9th, bases loaded, 1 out: Carson Kelly doubles (14) on a fly ball to center fielder Garrett Mitchell. Nico Hoerner scores. Pedro Ramírez scores. Michael Conforto to 3rd. Our side: win probability 28% -> 5% (-23%).
Worst half-inning: top 9th, 2 RBI -- win probability 50% -> 10% (-40%).
Metric: win_probability -- this bet settles on the full-game winner, which is exactly what MLB's win-probability series predicts.
Pitcher on the mound was the bullpen, not the starter.

**Verdict: VARIANCE (mechanism_undetermined)**
- 1 mechanism check(s) recorded, none refuted, but 1 could not be decided by this game: ['starter_velocity_gap@rung0']
- the pivot came off a relief pitcher, not a starter the decision named
- settlement's own thesis_outcome: UNTESTED

### WIN (WON -- control): 56ba4bb647b80640 / h2h home (game 823901)

Thesis: Backing the home side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) starter_velocity_gap -- how far the opposing starter's fastball sits above league pace (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 2.74 mph, home -0.53 mph (this game's own values rest on: away over 251 measured fastballs; home over 228 measured fastballs); a gap of 3.27 mph, clearing the threshold of 1.96 mph (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 3,149 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at -1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism harms), so the signal points to the home side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter whose fastball sits above league pace is holding stuff the season line has not caught up to, so the lineup facing the harder thrower is the disadvantaged side; velocity leads results and the market prices results. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 56ba4bb647b80640.)

**What happened.**
CIN 2 at LAD 3; 69 plate appearances, 0 lead change(s).
Stopped being in doubt in the top of the 5th -- MLB win probability: the last play after which the winner's own win probability never fell below 50% again.

**What the pick promised, and what the game did.**
- UNDETERMINED `starter_velocity_gap@rung0`: promised the harder-throwing starter -- the one this side is backed BECAUSE its opponent has to face him -- holds that opposing lineup below the league rate starters allow (rule: reached_base_rate < 0.3245, over at least 9 observations); measured unmeasurable over 0 observation(s) of no play-by-play stored -- below the frozen sample floor or unmeasurable.

**The pivot.**
top 3rd, runner on 1B, 2 out: Elly De La Cruz homers (25) on a fly ball to left center field. Dane Myers scores. Our side: win probability 74% -> 54% (-20%).
Worst half-inning: top 3rd, 2 RBI -- win probability 71% -> 56% (-16%).
Metric: win_probability -- this bet settles on the full-game winner, which is exactly what MLB's win-probability series predicts.
Pitcher on the mound was the starter.

**Verdict: VARIANCE (mechanism_undetermined)**
- 1 mechanism check(s) recorded, none refuted, but 1 could not be decided by this game: ['starter_velocity_gap@rung0']
- settlement's own thesis_outcome: UNTESTED

**What this post-mortem could not determine.**
- this decision's frozen mechanism check(s) could not be decided by this game -- too few plate appearances against the starter, or no play-by-play stored. The claim was made and stands unchecked; that is not the same as it holding

### WIN (WON -- control): 56ba4bb647b80640 / h2h away (game 824551)

Thesis: Backing the away side of h2h because 1 pre-registered signal fired: (1) starter_velocity_gap -- how far the opposing starter's fastball sits above league pace (each side's number describes the matchup that side's own lineup faces): away 0.41 mph, home 3.67 mph (this game's own values rest on: away over 219 measured fastballs; home over 265 measured fastballs); a gap of 3.26 mph, clearing the threshold of 1.96 mph (rung 1 of 3, the 50th percentile of that gap over the ladder's derivation sample, 3,149 games with both sides measured, 2023-24). This mechanism's direction was frozen before any search ran at -1 (the side holding more of it is the side the mechanism harms), so the signal points to the away side. Pre-registered reason it should matter: a starter whose fastball sits above league pace is holding stuff the season line has not caught up to, so the lineup facing the harder thrower is the disadvantaged side; velocity leads results and the market prices results. This is a signal count, not a forecast: the genome publishes no calibrated probability, so p_model is null and edge_bps is null by construction. Nothing here says the market's price is mistaken, and no claim of value is attached to it -- no such claim has been earned (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md). (Strategy: evolab genome 56ba4bb647b80640.)

**What happened.**
PIT 9 at CWS 3; 78 plate appearances, 0 lead change(s).
Stopped being in doubt in the top of the 1st -- MLB win probability: the last play after which the winner's own win probability never fell below 50% again.

**What the pick promised, and what the game did.**
- UNDETERMINED `starter_velocity_gap@rung0`: promised the harder-throwing starter -- the one this side is backed BECAUSE its opponent has to face him -- holds that opposing lineup below the league rate starters allow (rule: reached_base_rate < 0.3245, over at least 9 observations); measured unmeasurable over 0 observation(s) of no play-by-play stored -- below the frozen sample floor or unmeasurable.

**The pivot.**
top 1st, runners on 1B and 3B, 2 out: Konnor Griffin flies out to center fielder Tristan Peters. Our side: win probability 88% -> 86% (-2%).
Worst half-inning: top 3rd, 0 RBI -- win probability 96% -> 96% (-1%).
Metric: win_probability -- this bet settles on the full-game winner, which is exactly what MLB's win-probability series predicts.
Pitcher on the mound was the starter.

**Verdict: VARIANCE (mechanism_undetermined)**
- 1 mechanism check(s) recorded, none refuted, but 1 could not be decided by this game: ['starter_velocity_gap@rung0']
- settlement's own thesis_outcome: UNTESTED

**What this post-mortem could not determine.**
- this decision's frozen mechanism check(s) could not be decided by this game -- too few plate appearances against the starter, or no play-by-play stored. The claim was made and stands unchecked; that is not the same as it holding
